# Athletic Off-Season
_Your Blaze Camp plan_

- **Main goal:** Athletic performance
- **Sport:** American football - tight end / linebacker (TE / LB, FB, box S) · Early off-season
- **Level:** Advanced (L3): 3 years of consistent training, training 4 days a week now
- **Plan type:** 12 weeks (recommended) · Week 1-12
- **Start date:** 2027-01-11
- **Training days per week:** 4 · Monday, Tuesday, Thursday, Friday · Session length (minutes): 90
- Release accepted (Liability release (client), English)

## Before you start

- Your schedule cannot fit 10 weekly sets for Glutes, Side delts: add a training day
- Starting sets were lowered for Glutes, Chest, Side delts, Calves so the stairway fits your 90-minute sessions.
- Monday runs about 92 min (you picked 90): the minimum work of that day (2-4 exercises per muscle group, core, the athletic pieces) does not fit; see your schedule options
- Tuesday runs about 102 min (you picked 90): the minimum work of that day (2-4 exercises per muscle group, core, the athletic pieces) does not fit; see your schedule options

**Your schedule options**
- Keep 90 minutes: lower starting sets, same stairway (this plan)
- Longer sessions (100 min) with the full starting sets
- One more training day (5 days)

## Basic Instruction
> \*Always leave 1 rep in the tank, never work up to failure but get as close as you can.
>
> \*\*Do steady cardio whenever you want, just don't overdo it... I recommend doing it on your aerobic days, max 40 min of steady.
>
> \*\*\*The lower the reps, the higher the weight... Always go for 1-3 RIR (reps in reserve), aka RPE 7+.
>
> \*\*\*\*When there are joint pains, usually going unilateral and/or getting away from fixed grips (barbells, bars) and going to split grips (dumbbells, kettlebells, individual grips) helps take care of some stuff.
>
> \*\*\*\*\*If you have any questions just ask me - IG @BLAZECAMP or TWITTER @BLAZECAMP\_

Planned effort across the stairway: weeks 1-6: leave 3 in the tank; weeks 7-9: leave 2 in the tank; weeks 10-11: leave 1-2 in the tank; week 12: leave 1 in the tank. The last week may add one intensifier on a small-muscle exercise.

How heavy? Each exercise tells you how many reps to leave in the tank: "leave 2 in the tank" means you could do 2 more reps at the end of the set (about RPE 8; 3 left = RPE 7, 1 left = RPE 9). The first weeks leave more in the tank, the last weeks less. Week 1: start a little lighter, with a weight you could lift 2-3 more times.

Olympic lifts are for power. Cleans, snatches and their dumbbell and kettlebell versions train power: move the weight as fast as you can with clean technique. Pick a weight you can move explosively on every rep; if it slows down or your technique breaks, it's too heavy. You don't need percentages for these unless you compete in Olympic weightlifting.

Your plan climbs like a stairway: small steps first, a bit bigger after the halfway week, the hardest weeks at the end.

## Week 1 — Getting used to it (small steps)

_Effort level this week: 2 (2 = start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

**Baseline test this week (before you load up)** (Strength, Speed / agility, Jumps)

**Athletic work this week:** 146 jump contacts · 72 throws · 80 direction changes · 240 m of sprints · 8 Olympic-lift sets · Plyo share 0% · Receive-Create 1: Land

### Monday · A1 - squat + pull
_about 92 min_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **A-March (running drill)**
   - Swap for: A-Skip (running drill) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=A-March%20(running%20drill))

**Core**
1. **Sit-Up** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Copenhagen Plank
   - Optional: add a core superset to any exercise
   - Swap for: Crunch / V-Up / Plank (forearm / low plank) / Weighted Sit-Up
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Sit-Up)
2. **Copenhagen Plank** — 2 × 20-40 s · hold with good form, stop before it breaks · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Sit-Up
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Hanging Rotational Knees-to-Chest (hanging oblique knee raise) / Toes to Bar (hanging) / Knees to Chest (hanging) / Hanging Rotational Knee/Leg Raise
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=aDsaGBnvDQo)

**Test**
1. 10-30 m sprint — 10-30 m sprint · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - **Test week:** 10-30 m sprint
   - Test: 2-3 timed runs with full rest, record the best.
   - Baseline test this week: it sets your starting numbers.
2. **5-10-5 Pro-Agility Shuttle** — 5-10-5 Pro-Agility Shuttle · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - **Test week:** 5-10-5 Pro-Agility Shuttle
   - Test: 2-3 timed runs with full rest, record the best.
   - Baseline test this week: it sets your starting numbers.
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=5-10-5%20Pro-Agility%20Shuttle)
3. countermovement jump — countermovement jump · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - **Test week:** countermovement jump
   - Test: 3 attempts, record the best.
   - Baseline test this week: it sets your starting numbers.

**Jumps, throws and speed**
1. **Max-Velocity Sprint (Top Speed)** — 2 × 6 x 20 m · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Swap for: A-Skip (running drill) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill) / A-March (running drill) / Falling-Start Accelerations
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Max-Velocity%20Sprint%20(Top%20Speed))
2. **Low Box Jump (Half-Shin Height)** — 3 × 8 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · work:rest 1:5 to 1:10
   - low box (6-12 in)
   - Land and stick it: hold 3 s
   - Swap for: Hex-Bar Jump Squat / High Depth Drop (Above-Hip Height) / Medium Depth Drop / Low Depth Drop
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Low%20Box%20Jump%20(Half-Shin%20Height))
3. **Bilateral Zig-Zag Ankle Hops** — 2 × 25 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · work:rest 1:5 to 1:10
   - Land and stick it: hold 3 s
   - Swap for: Bilateral Right-Angle Ankle Hops (Forward-Right-Forward-Left-Forward) / Ankle Hops (bilateral, in place) / Bilateral Backward Ankle Hops / Bilateral Lateral Ankle Hops
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Bilateral%20Zig-Zag%20Ankle%20Hops)
4. **Step-Forward Medball Overhead Throw (R & L leg)** — 3 × 12 Reps · max intent; stop when quality drops · 45-90 s rest
   - 6-10 lb ball
   > No levels: just do them right (quality and intent over load).
   - Swap for: Medball Half-Kneeling Rotational Scoop - Right-Facing, Right Leg Forward / Medball Half-Kneeling Rotational Scoop - Left-Facing, Left Leg Up / Medball Sit-Up to Chest Pass / Medball Chest Pass
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=i9M9ZnSFEw4)
5. **Comeback / Curl Route** — 2 × 10 cuts · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Swap for: Flat Route / Fade Route / Slant Route / Post / Corner Route
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Comeback%20%2F%20Curl%20Route)

**Power / Olympic lifts**
1. **Squat Clean & Jerk** — 2 × 3 to 5 Reps · move it fast: if the bar slows down or your technique breaks, it is too heavy · 2-3 min rest
   - weight by speed: fast and powerful
   - Full squat catch: 3-5 reps per set, every rep fast
   - Olympic lifts are for power: no percentages unless your coach switches them on
   > Full squat catch.
   - Swap for: Power Clean / Squat Clean / Power Clean & Jerk / Low-Hang Clean Pull
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Squat%20Clean%20%26%20Jerk)

**Strength**
1. **Front Squat (barbell)** — 3 × 3 rep max or work up to a 1 rep max · max intent; stop when quality drops · 3-6 min rest
   - **Test week:** 3 rep max or work up to a 1 rep max
   - Reps capped for this exercise (7-10): it is harder than its group.
   - Test: work up to one top set of 3 rep max or work up to a 1 rep max (warm-up sets first), then record the weight.
   - Baseline test this week: it sets your starting numbers.
   - Swap for: Box Squat - any variation, dead-stop ('Deload' = sit and unload on the box) / Low-Bar Back Squat / High-Bar Back Squat / Zercher Squat (barbell)
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/uYumuL_G_V0)
2. **Single-Arm DB/KB Row** — 3 × 12 to 15 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Week 1: pick a weight you could lift 2-3 more times
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Single-Arm Cable Row / Seal Row - Barbell (Pronated Grip) / Seal Row - DB (Supinated Grip) / Seal Row - Barbell (Supinated Grip)
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xl1YiqQY2vA)
3. **Staggered-Stance (Kickstand) DB Deadlift** — 2 × 12 to 15 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Standing Barbell Overhead Press (strict)
   - Week 1: pick a weight you could lift 2-3 more times
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Rear-Foot-on-Wall DB Deadlift (RDL) / Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB) / Staggered-Stance Deadlift / Single-Leg RDL / Single-Leg Deadlift
   - [Video](https://youtube.com/shorts/avUPBq-FrDU?feature=share)
4. **Standing Barbell Overhead Press (strict)** — 3 × 7-10 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Staggered-Stance (Kickstand) DB Deadlift
   - Week 1: pick a weight you could lift 2-3 more times
   - Reps capped for this exercise (7-10): it is harder than its group.
   - Swap for: Barbell Push Jerk / Barbell Push Press / Standing DB Overhead Press / DB Z-Press (seated on floor)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Standing%20Barbell%20Overhead%20Press%20(strict))
5. **Weighted Pull-Up** — 2 × 5-8 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Sissy Squat
   - Week 1: pick a weight you could lift 2-3 more times
   - Reps capped for this exercise (5-8): it is harder than its group.
   - Swap for: Chin-Up (supinated) / L-Sit Pull-Up / Strict Pull-Up (Knees to Chest / tuck) / Wide-Grip Pull-Up
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Weighted%20Pull-Up)

**Accessories**
1. **Sissy Squat** — 2 × 12 to 15 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Weighted Pull-Up
   - Swap for: Leg Extension (machine) / DB Leg Extension (seated on box)
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/HW5yUsjKru4)
2. **Nordic Hamstring Curl** — 2 × 5-8 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest
   - Reps capped for this exercise (5-8): it is harder than its group.
   - Swap for: Prone Dumbbell Hamstring Curl (DB between feet) / Supine Slider Hamstring Curl / Ball Hamstring Curl (medball or stability ball) / Machine Leg Curl (seated or lying)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Nordic%20Hamstring%20Curl)
3. **Seated (Chest-to-Thighs) DB Reverse Fly** — 4 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly / KB Face Pull / Floor Prone Y / T Raises (bodyweight or light DBs) / Face Pull
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seated%20(Chest-to-Thighs)%20DB%20Reverse%20Fly)
4. **Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional)** — 3 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: Barbell Calf Raise / Single-Leg Deficit Calf Raise (weighted) / Seated / Bent-Knee Calf Raise (soleus; machine or DBs on the knees) / Machine Calf Raise
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Standing%20DB%20Calf%20Raise%20(deficit%20optional))

**Strength**
1. **Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB)** — 3 × 12 to 15 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Week 1: pick a weight you could lift 2-3 more times
   - per side = 1 set
   > Hold the DBs (or a barbell), take a half step back into a staggered stance and deadlift on the front leg; switch legs and keep alternating every rep. Saves time compared with finishing one side first.
   - Swap for: Staggered-Stance (Kickstand) DB Deadlift / Rear-Foot-on-Wall DB Deadlift (RDL) / Staggered-Stance Deadlift / Single-Leg RDL / Single-Leg Deadlift
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Alternating%20Staggered-Stance%20Single-Leg%20Deadlift%20(DB))
2. **Barbell Bench Press** — 3 × 3 rep max or work up to a 1 rep max · max intent; stop when quality drops · 3-6 min rest
   - **Test week:** 3 rep max or work up to a 1 rep max
   - Reps capped for this exercise (7-10): it is harder than its group.
   - Test: work up to one top set of 3 rep max or work up to a 1 rep max (warm-up sets first), then record the weight.
   - Baseline test this week: it sets your starting numbers.
   - Swap for: Incline Barbell Bench Press / Alternating Flat DB Bench Press / Alternating DB Floor Press / Alternating Incline DB Bench Press (one up / one down)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Barbell%20Bench%20Press)

   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Step-Up (forward and lateral) / Stationary Lunge (Split Squat) / Rear-Foot-Against-Wall Split Squat / Front-Foot-Elevated Split Squat / Lunge (FFE)
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/zKWe7UAWbIo)

## Week 2 — Getting used to it (small steps)

_Effort level this week: 2.6 (2 = start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

**What changes this week**
- +1 set on Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly (Thursday)
- Glutes is at its weekly ceiling: add load or reps, not sets
- Side delts is at its weekly ceiling: add load or reps, not sets
- Same sets: add a little load or reps this week

**Athletic work this week:** 146 jump contacts · 72 throws · 80 direction changes · 252 m of sprints · 8 Olympic-lift sets · Plyo share 0% · Receive-Create 1: Land

## Week 3 — Driving it (steps get a bit bigger)

_Effort level this week: 3.2 (2 = start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

**What changes this week**
- New bonus exercise: Machine Reverse Fly (rear-delt pec deck) (Thursday)
- Same sets: add a little load or reps this week

**Athletic work this week:** 146 jump contacts · 81 throws · 88 direction changes · 276 m of sprints · 8 Olympic-lift sets · Plyo share 0% · Receive-Create 1: Land

   > Full squat catch.
   - Swap for: Power Clean / Squat Clean / Power Clean & Jerk / Low-Hang Clean Pull
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Squat%20Clean%20%26%20Jerk)

**Strength**
1. **Front Squat (barbell)** — 3 × 7-10 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Single-Arm DB/KB Row
   - Reps capped for this exercise (7-10): it is harder than its group.
   - Swap for: Box Squat - any variation, dead-stop ('Deload' = sit and unload on the box) / Low-Bar Back Squat / High-Bar Back Squat / Zercher Squat (barbell)
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/uYumuL_G_V0)
2. **Single-Arm DB/KB Row** — 3 × 12 to 15 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Front Squat (barbell)
   - per side = 1 set

   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xl1YiqQY2vA)
3. **Staggered-Stance (Kickstand) DB Deadlift** — 2 × 12 to 15 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Standing Barbell Overhead Press (strict)
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Rear-Foot-on-Wall DB Deadlift (RDL) / Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB) / Staggered-Stance Deadlift / Single-Leg RDL / Single-Leg Deadlift

4. **Standing Barbell Overhead Press (strict)** — 3 × 7-10 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Staggered-Stance (Kickstand) DB Deadlift
   - Reps capped for this exercise (7-10): it is harder than its group.
   - Swap for: Barbell Push Jerk / Barbell Push Press / Standing DB Overhead Press / DB Z-Press (seated on floor)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Standing%20Barbell%20Overhead%20Press%20(strict))
5. **Weighted Pull-Up** — 2 × 5-8 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Sissy Squat
   - Reps capped for this exercise (5-8): it is harder than its group.

## Week 4 — Driving it (steps get a bit bigger)

_Effort level this week: 3.8 (2 = start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

**What changes this week**
- Jumps move to Receive-Create 2: Land & Go
- +1 set on Sissy Squat (Monday)
- Same sets: add a little load or reps this week

**Athletic work this week:** 122 jump contacts · 84 throws · 88 direction changes · 288 m of sprints · 8 Olympic-lift sets · Plyo share 35% · Receive-Create 2: Land & Go

### Monday · A1 - squat + pull
_about 94 min_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **A-March (running drill)**
   - Swap for: A-Skip (running drill) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=A-March%20(running%20drill))

**Core**
1. **Sit-Up** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Copenhagen Plank
   - Swap for: Crunch / V-Up / Plank (forearm / low plank) / Weighted Sit-Up
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Sit-Up)
2. **Copenhagen Plank** — 2 × 20-40 s · hold with good form, stop before it breaks · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Sit-Up
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Hanging Rotational Knees-to-Chest (hanging oblique knee raise) / Toes to Bar (hanging) / Knees to Chest (hanging) / Hanging Rotational Knee/Leg Raise
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=aDsaGBnvDQo)

**Jumps, throws and speed**
1. **Max-Velocity Sprint (Top Speed)** — 2 × 6 x 24 m · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Swap for: A-Skip (running drill) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill) / A-March (running drill) / Falling-Start Accelerations
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Max-Velocity%20Sprint%20(Top%20Speed))
2. **Single-Leg Depth Jump** — 3 × 6 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · 2-3 min rest
   - low box (6-12 in)
   - Land, then go: one jump or hop right after the landing
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Single-Leg DB Depth Drop, Release DBs, Jump / Depth Drop to Box Jump / Depth Jump / DB Depth Drop, Release DBs, Jump
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Single-Leg%20Depth%20Jump)
3. **Bilateral Right-Angle Ankle Hops (Forward-Right-Forward-Left-Forward)** — 2 × 25 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · work:rest 1:5 to 1:10
   - Land, then go: one jump or hop right after the landing
   - Swap for: Bilateral Zig-Zag Ankle Hops / Ankle Hops (bilateral, in place) / Bilateral Backward Ankle Hops / Bilateral Lateral Ankle Hops
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Bilateral%20Right-Angle%20Ankle%20Hops%20(Forward-Right-Forward-Left-Forward))
4. **Step-Forward Medball Overhead Throw (R & L leg)** — 3 × 14 Reps · max intent; stop when quality drops · 45-90 s rest
   - 6-10 lb ball
   > No levels: just do them right (quality and intent over load).
   - Swap for: Medball Half-Kneeling Rotational Scoop - Right-Facing, Right Leg Forward / Medball Half-Kneeling Rotational Scoop - Left-Facing, Left Leg Up / Medball Sit-Up to Chest Pass / Medball Chest Pass
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=i9M9ZnSFEw4)
5. **Comeback / Curl Route** — 2 × 11 cuts · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Swap for: Flat Route / Fade Route / Slant Route / Post / Corner Route
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Comeback%20%2F%20Curl%20Route)

**Power / Olympic lifts**
1. **Squat Clean & Jerk** — 2 × 3 to 5 Reps · move it fast: if the bar slows down or your technique breaks, it is too heavy · 2-3 min rest
   - weight by speed: fast and powerful
   - Full squat catch: 3-5 reps per set, every rep fast
   - Olympic lifts are for power: no percentages unless your coach switches them on
   > Full squat catch.
   - Swap for: Power Clean / Squat Clean / Power Clean & Jerk / Low-Hang Clean Pull
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Squat%20Clean%20%26%20Jerk)

**Strength**
1. **Front Squat (barbell)** — 3 × 7-10 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Single-Arm DB/KB Row
   - Reps capped for this exercise (7-10): it is harder than its group.
   - Swap for: Box Squat - any variation, dead-stop ('Deload' = sit and unload on the box) / Low-Bar Back Squat / High-Bar Back Squat / Zercher Squat (barbell)
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/uYumuL_G_V0)
2. **Single-Arm DB/KB Row** — 3 × 12 to 15 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Front Squat (barbell)
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Single-Arm Cable Row / Seal Row - Barbell (Pronated Grip) / Seal Row - DB (Supinated Grip) / Seal Row - Barbell (Supinated Grip)
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xl1YiqQY2vA)
3. **Staggered-Stance (Kickstand) DB Deadlift** — 2 × 12 to 15 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Standing Barbell Overhead Press (strict)
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Rear-Foot-on-Wall DB Deadlift (RDL) / Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB) / Staggered-Stance Deadlift / Single-Leg RDL / Single-Leg Deadlift
   - [Video](https://youtube.com/shorts/avUPBq-FrDU?feature=share)
4. **Standing Barbell Overhead Press (strict)** — 3 × 7-10 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Staggered-Stance (Kickstand) DB Deadlift
   - Reps capped for this exercise (7-10): it is harder than its group.
   - Swap for: Barbell Push Jerk / Barbell Push Press / Standing DB Overhead Press / DB Z-Press (seated on floor)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Standing%20Barbell%20Overhead%20Press%20(strict))
5. **Weighted Pull-Up** — 2 × 5-8 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Sissy Squat
   - Reps capped for this exercise (5-8): it is harder than its group.
   - Swap for: Chin-Up (supinated) / L-Sit Pull-Up / Strict Pull-Up (Knees to Chest / tuck) / Wide-Grip Pull-Up
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Weighted%20Pull-Up)

**Accessories**
1. **Sissy Squat** — 3 × 12 to 15 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Weighted Pull-Up
   - Swap for: Leg Extension (machine) / DB Leg Extension (seated on box)
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/HW5yUsjKru4)
2. **Nordic Hamstring Curl** — 2 × 5-8 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest
   - Reps capped for this exercise (5-8): it is harder than its group.
   - Swap for: Prone Dumbbell Hamstring Curl (DB between feet) / Supine Slider Hamstring Curl / Ball Hamstring Curl (medball or stability ball) / Machine Leg Curl (seated or lying)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Nordic%20Hamstring%20Curl)
3. **Seated (Chest-to-Thighs) DB Reverse Fly** — 4 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly / KB Face Pull / Floor Prone Y / T Raises (bodyweight or light DBs) / Face Pull
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seated%20(Chest-to-Thighs)%20DB%20Reverse%20Fly)
4. **Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional)** — 3 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: Barbell Calf Raise / Single-Leg Deficit Calf Raise (weighted) / Seated / Bent-Knee Calf Raise (soleus; machine or DBs on the knees) / Machine Calf Raise
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Standing%20DB%20Calf%20Raise%20(deficit%20optional))

## Week 5 — Driving it (steps get a bit bigger)

_Effort level this week: 4.4 (2 = start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

**What changes this week**
- New exercise block: one small change on some exercises
- +1 set on Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) (Monday)
- Same sets: add a little load or reps this week

**Athletic work this week:** 146 jump contacts · 84 throws · 88 direction changes · 300 m of sprints · 8 Olympic-lift sets · Plyo share 21% · Receive-Create 2: Land & Go

### Monday · A1 - squat + pull
_about 95 min_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **A-March (running drill)**
   - Swap for: A-Skip (running drill) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=A-March%20(running%20drill))

**Core**
1. **Sit-Up** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Copenhagen Plank
   - Swap for: Crunch / V-Up / Plank (forearm / low plank) / Weighted Sit-Up
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Sit-Up)
2. **Copenhagen Plank** — 2 × 20-40 s · hold with good form, stop before it breaks · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Sit-Up
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Hanging Rotational Knees-to-Chest (hanging oblique knee raise) / Toes to Bar (hanging) / Knees to Chest (hanging) / Hanging Rotational Knee/Leg Raise
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=aDsaGBnvDQo)

**Jumps, throws and speed**

   - Swap for: A-Skip (running drill) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill) / A-March (running drill) / Falling-Start Accelerations
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Max-Velocity%20Sprint%20(Top%20Speed))
2. **Single-Leg Depth Jump** — 3 × 10 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · 2-3 min rest
   - low box (6-12 in)

   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Single-Leg DB Depth Drop, Release DBs, Jump / Depth Drop to Box Jump / Depth Jump / DB Depth Drop, Release DBs, Jump
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Single-Leg%20Depth%20Jump)

   - Land, then go: one jump or hop right after the landing
   - Swap for: Bilateral Right-Angle Ankle Hops (Forward-Right-Forward-Left-Forward) / Ankle Hops (bilateral, in place) / Bilateral Backward Ankle Hops / Bilateral Lateral Ankle Hops

**Power / Olympic lifts**

**Athletic work this week:** 146 jump contacts · 93 throws · 88 direction changes · 324 m of sprints · 8 Olympic-lift sets · Plyo share 20% · Receive-Create 2: Land & Go

   - Swap for: Side (Lateral) Lunge / Deficit Reverse Lunge (standing on plate/step) / Rear-Foot-Elevated Lunge (RFE / Bulgarian split squat) / Front-Foot-Elevated Split Squat / Lunge (FFE)
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/9ZknEYboBOQ)
2. **Bent-Over Barbell Row (Supinated / Yates grip)** — 3 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Step-Up (forward and lateral)
   - Swap for: Single-Arm DB/KB Row / Single-Arm Cable Row / Bent-Over Barbell Row / Renegade Row
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Bent-Over%20Barbell%20Row%20(Supinated%20%2F%20Yates%20grip))
3. **Seesaw DB Press (alternating, one up / one down)** — 3 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Alternating DB Overhead Press / Half-Kneeling Single-Arm Landmine Press / Standing Barbell Overhead Press (strict) / Barbell Push Press
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seesaw%20DB%20Press%20(alternating%2C%20one%20up%20%2F%20one%20down))
4. **L-Sit Pull-Up** — 2 × 8-10 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ DB Leg Extension (seated on box)
   - Reps capped for this exercise (8-10): it is harder than its group.
   - Body-weight lift: if you cannot reach the reps, use the assisted machine or a band-free regression (feet on the floor, ring or TRX row).
   - Swap for: Strict Pull-Up (Knees to Chest / tuck) / Weighted Pull-Up / Strict Pull-Up (Feet Behind) / Wide-Grip Pull-Up
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=L-Sit%20Pull-Up)

**Accessories**
1. **DB Leg Extension (seated on box)** — 2 × 10 to 12 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ L-Sit Pull-Up
   - Swap for: Sissy Squat / Leg Extension (machine)
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/HpCE66lfEuk)
2. **Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly** — 4 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: Seated (Chest-to-Thighs) DB Reverse Fly / KB Face Pull / Floor Prone Y / T Raises (bodyweight or light DBs) / Face Pull
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Bent-Over%20DB%20Reverse%20Fly)
3. **Standing Calf Raise (bodyweight, from a deficit/step)** — 3 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: Barbell Calf Raise / Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) / Single-Leg Deficit Calf Raise (weighted) / Machine Calf Raise
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Standing%20Calf%20Raise%20(bodyweight%2C%20from%20a%20deficit%2Fstep))
4. **Machine Reverse Fly (rear-delt pec deck) (bonus)** — 1 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest
   - Bonus exercise to reach your weekly sets
   - Swap for: Seated (Chest-to-Thighs) DB Reverse Fly / KB Face Pull / Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly / Floor Prone Y / T Raises (bodyweight or light DBs)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Machine%20Reverse%20Fly%20(rear-delt%20pec%20deck))

## Week 7 — Driving it (steps get a bit bigger)

_Effort level this week: 6 (2 = start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

**What changes this week**
- +1 set on Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB) (Tuesday)
- Same sets: add a little load or reps this week

**Athletic work this week:** 146 jump contacts · 93 throws · 88 direction changes · 348 m of sprints · 8 Olympic-lift sets · Plyo share 20% · Receive-Create 2: Land & Go

### Monday · A1 - squat + pull
_about 98 min_

**Warm-up**

   - Full squat catch: 3-5 reps per set, every rep fast

## Week 8 — Driving it (steps get a bit bigger)

_Effort level this week: 7 (2 = start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

**What changes this week**
- Jumps move to Receive-Create 3: Repeat
- +1 set on Side (Lateral) Lunge (Tuesday)
- +1 set on Cable Fly (Tuesday)
- +1 set on Machine Reverse Fly (rear-delt pec deck) (Thursday)
- +1 set on Barbell Calf Raise (Tuesday)
- Same sets: add a little load or reps this week

**Athletic work this week:** 134 jump contacts · 96 throws · 96 direction changes · 372 m of sprints · 8 Olympic-lift sets · Plyo share 37% · Receive-Create 3: Repeat

## Week 9 — Hardest weeks

_Effort level this week: 8 (2 = start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

**What changes this week**
- New exercise block: one small change on some exercises
- +1 set on Machine Leg Curl (seated or lying) (Friday)
- Same sets: add a little load or reps this week

**Athletic work this week:** 131 jump contacts · 105 throws · 96 direction changes · 330 m of sprints · 8 Olympic-lift sets · Plyo share 34% · Receive-Create 3: Repeat

## Week 10 — Hardest weeks

_Effort level this week: 9 (2 = start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

**What changes this week**
- +1 set on Strict Pull-Up (Knees to Chest / tuck) (Thursday)
- +1 set on Side (Lateral) Lunge (Thursday)
- +1 set on Machine Reverse Fly (rear-delt pec deck) (Thursday)
- +1 set on Standing Calf Raise (bodyweight, from a deficit/step) (Thursday)
- Same sets: add a little load or reps this week

**Athletic work this week:** 131 jump contacts · 105 throws · 96 direction changes · 360 m of sprints · 8 Olympic-lift sets · Plyo share 34% · Receive-Create 3: Repeat

   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Squat%20Clean%20%26%20Jerk)

**Strength**
1. **Front Squat (barbell)** — 3 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (1-2 RIR, RPE 8-9) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Single-Arm Cable Row
   - Swap for: Box Squat - any variation, dead-stop ('Deload' = sit and unload on the box) / Low-Bar Back Squat / High-Bar Back Squat / Zercher Squat (barbell)
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/uYumuL_G_V0)
2. **Single-Arm Cable Row** — 4 × 6 to 8 per side · leave 1-2 in the tank (1-2 RIR, RPE 8-9) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Front Squat (barbell)
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Single-Arm DB/KB Row / Seal Row - Barbell (Pronated Grip) / Seal Row - DB (Supinated Grip) / Seal Row - Barbell (Supinated Grip)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Single-Arm%20Cable%20Row)
3. **Staggered-Stance (Kickstand) DB Deadlift** — 2 × 6 to 8 per side · leave 1-2 in the tank (1-2 RIR, RPE 8-9) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Barbell Push Jerk
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Rear-Foot-on-Wall DB Deadlift (RDL) / Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB) / Staggered-Stance Deadlift / Single-Leg RDL / Single-Leg Deadlift
   - [Video](https://youtube.com/shorts/avUPBq-FrDU?feature=share)
4. **Barbell Push Jerk** — 3 × 3 to 5 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (1-2 RIR, RPE 8-9) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Staggered-Stance (Kickstand) DB Deadlift
   - Reps capped for this exercise (3-5): it is harder than its group.
   - Swap for: Standing Barbell Overhead Press (strict) / Barbell Push Press / Standing DB Overhead Press / DB Z-Press (seated on floor)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Barbell%20Push%20Jerk)
5. **Lat Pulldown (Close Grip)** — 2 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (1-2 RIR, RPE 8-9) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Sissy Squat
   - Swap for: Weighted Pull-Up / Chin-Up (supinated) / L-Sit Pull-Up / Strict Pull-Up (Knees to Chest / tuck)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Lat%20Pulldown%20(Close%20Grip))

**Accessories**
1. **Sissy Squat** — 3 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (1-2 RIR, RPE 8-9) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Lat Pulldown (Close Grip)
   - Swap for: Leg Extension (machine) / DB Leg Extension (seated on box)
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/HW5yUsjKru4)
2. **Prone Dumbbell Hamstring Curl (DB between feet)** — 2 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (1-2 RIR, RPE 8-9) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: Nordic Hamstring Curl / Supine Slider Hamstring Curl / Ball Hamstring Curl (medball or stability ball) / Machine Leg Curl (seated or lying)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Prone%20Dumbbell%20Hamstring%20Curl%20(DB%20between%20feet))
3. **Seated (Chest-to-Thighs) DB Reverse Fly** — 4 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (1-2 RIR, RPE 8-9) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly / KB Face Pull / Floor Prone Y / T Raises (bodyweight or light DBs) / Face Pull
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seated%20(Chest-to-Thighs)%20DB%20Reverse%20Fly)
4. **Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional)** — 4 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (1-2 RIR, RPE 8-9) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: Barbell Calf Raise / Single-Leg Deficit Calf Raise (weighted) / Seated / Bent-Knee Calf Raise (soleus; machine or DBs on the knees) / Machine Calf Raise
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Standing%20DB%20Calf%20Raise%20(deficit%20optional))

### Tuesday · B1 - hip hinge + push
_about 96 min_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **A-March (running drill)**
   - Swap for: A-Skip (running drill) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=A-March%20(running%20drill))

**Core**
1. **Weighted Russian Twist** — 2 × 10 to 15 per side · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 30-60 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Cable Oblique Rotation (cable rotational twist) / Cable Woodchopper (cable chop) / Russian Twist / Side Plank (elbow)
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4p5jWyz-YRQ)
2. **Toes to Bar (hanging)** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 30-60 s rest
   - Swap for: Knees to Chest (hanging) / V-Up / Leg Raise (lying / supine) / Reverse Crunch
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Toes%20to%20Bar%20(hanging))

**Jumps, throws and speed**
1. **Depth Jump** — 3 × 6 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · 2-3 min rest
   - low box (6-12 in)
   - Repeat: quick, continuous contacts
   - Swap for: Single-Leg Forward Hurdle Hops / Hurdle Hops (tall hurdles, continuous quick-contact reps) / Single-Leg Squirrel Jumps / Single-Leg Plate Hops
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Depth%20Jump)
2. **Dig / Out Route** — 3 × 12 cuts · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Swap for: Lateral Shuffles / Slant Route / Flat Route / Fade Route
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Dig%20%2F%20Out%20Route)

**Power / Olympic lifts**
1. **Power Snatch** — 2 × 3 to 5 Reps · move it fast: if the bar slows down or your technique breaks, it is too heavy · 2-3 min rest
   - weight by speed: fast and powerful
   - Olympic lifts are for power: no percentages unless your coach switches them on
   - Swap for: Squat Snatch / Low-Hang Snatch Pull / Single-Arm DB Snatch / High-Hang Squat Snatch
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Power%20Snatch)

**Strength**
1. **Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB)** — 4 × 6 to 8 per side · leave 1-2 in the tank (1-2 RIR, RPE 8-9) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Barbell Bench Press
   - per side = 1 set
   > Hold the DBs (or a barbell), take a half step back into a staggered stance and deadlift on the front leg; switch legs and keep alternating every rep. Saves time compared with finishing one side first.
   - Swap for: Staggered-Stance (Kickstand) DB Deadlift / Rear-Foot-on-Wall DB Deadlift (RDL) / Staggered-Stance Deadlift / Single-Leg RDL / Single-Leg Deadlift
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Alternating%20Staggered-Stance%20Single-Leg%20Deadlift%20(DB))
2. **Barbell Bench Press** — 3 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (1-2 RIR, RPE 8-9) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB)
   - Swap for: Incline Barbell Bench Press / Alternating Flat DB Bench Press / Alternating DB Floor Press / Alternating Incline DB Bench Press (one up / one down)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Barbell%20Bench%20Press)
3. **Step-Up (forward and lateral)** — 3 × 6 to 8 per side · leave 1-2 in the tank (1-2 RIR, RPE 8-9) · rest after the pair
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Side (Lateral) Lunge / Stationary Lunge (Split Squat) / Rear-Foot-Against-Wall Split Squat / Front-Foot-Elevated Split Squat / Lunge (FFE)
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/9ZknEYboBOQ)

**Accessories**
1. **Nordic Hamstring Curl** — 2 × 5-8 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (1-2 RIR, RPE 8-9) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Cable Fly
   - Reps capped for this exercise (5-8): it is harder than its group.
   - Swap for: Prone Dumbbell Hamstring Curl (DB between feet) / Supine Slider Hamstring Curl / Ball Hamstring Curl (medball or stability ball) / Seated Leg Curl (machine)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Nordic%20Hamstring%20Curl)
2. **Cable Fly** — 3 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (1-2 RIR, RPE 8-9) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Nordic Hamstring Curl
   - Swap for: Flat DB Fly / Machine Fly (Pec Deck)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Cable%20Fly)
3. **Leg Extension (machine)** — 2 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (1-2 RIR, RPE 8-9) · rest after the pair
   - Swap for: Sissy Squat / DB Leg Extension (seated on box)
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/m0FOpMEgero)
4. **Barbell Calf Raise** — 4 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 1-2 in the tank (1-2 RIR, RPE 8-9) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) / Single-Leg Deficit Calf Raise (weighted) / Machine Calf Raise / Seated / Bent-Knee Calf Raise (soleus; machine or DBs on the knees)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Barbell%20Calf%20Raise)

**Aerobic**
1. **Aerobic A (blue): 15 Sec Run/ 45 Sec Walk** — Blue (A): short / high intensity · 15-20 min
   - Any cardio tool works: run, bike, rower or ski erg. Track time, calories or distance.
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Aerobic%20A%20(blue)%3A%2015%20Sec%20Run%2F%2045%20Sec%20Walk)

3. **Seesaw DB Press (alternating, one up / one down)** — 3 × 6 to 8 per side · leave 1-2 in the tank (1-2 RIR, RPE 8-9) · rest after the pair

## Week 11 — Hardest weeks

_Effort level this week: 10 (2 = start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

**What changes this week**
- +1 set on Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB) (Tuesday)
- Hamstrings is at its weekly ceiling: add load or reps, not sets
- Same sets: add a little load or reps this week

**Athletic work this week:** 131 jump contacts · 108 throws · 96 direction changes · 380 m of sprints · 8 Olympic-lift sets · Plyo share 34% · Receive-Create 3: Repeat

### Monday · A1 - squat + pull
_about 98 min_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **A-March (running drill)**
   - Swap for: A-Skip (running drill) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=A-March%20(running%20drill))

**Core**
1. **Sit-Up** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Copenhagen Plank
   - Swap for: Crunch / V-Up / Plank (forearm / low plank) / Weighted Sit-Up
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Sit-Up)
2. **Copenhagen Plank** — 2 × 20-40 s · hold with good form, stop before it breaks · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Sit-Up
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Hanging Rotational Knees-to-Chest (hanging oblique knee raise) / Toes to Bar (hanging) / Knees to Chest (hanging) / Hanging Rotational Knee/Leg Raise
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=aDsaGBnvDQo)

**Jumps, throws and speed**
1. **Max-Velocity Sprint (Top Speed)** — 2 × 5 x 38 m · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Swap for: A-Skip (running drill) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill) / A-March (running drill) / Falling-Start Accelerations
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Max-Velocity%20Sprint%20(Top%20Speed))
2. **Single-Leg Depth Jump** — 3 × 7 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · 2-3 min rest
   - low box (6-12 in)
   - Repeat: quick, continuous contacts
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Hurdle Hops (tall hurdles, continuous quick-contact reps) / Single-Leg Forward Hurdle Hops / Tuck Jumps / Single-Leg Plate Hops
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Single-Leg%20Depth%20Jump)
3. **Bilateral Zig-Zag Ankle Hops** — 2 × 25 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · work:rest 1:5 to 1:10
   - Repeat: quick, continuous contacts
   - Swap for: Bilateral Right-Angle Ankle Hops (Forward-Right-Forward-Left-Forward) / Ankle Hops (bilateral, in place) / Bilateral Backward Ankle Hops / Bilateral Lateral Ankle Hops
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Bilateral%20Zig-Zag%20Ankle%20Hops)
4. **Step-Forward Medball Overhead Throw (R & L leg)** — 3 × 18 Reps · max intent; stop when quality drops · 45-90 s rest
   - 6-10 lb ball
   > No levels: just do them right (quality and intent over load).
   - Swap for: Medball Half-Kneeling Rotational Scoop - Right-Facing, Right Leg Forward / Medball Half-Kneeling Rotational Scoop - Left-Facing, Left Leg Up / Medball Sit-Up to Chest Pass / Medball Chest Pass

   - Swap for: Flat Route / Fade Route / Slant Route / Post / Corner Route
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Comeback%20%2F%20Curl%20Route)

**Power / Olympic lifts**
1. **Squat Clean & Jerk** — 2 × 3 to 5 Reps · move it fast: if the bar slows down or your technique breaks, it is too heavy · 2-3 min rest
   - weight by speed: fast and powerful
   - Full squat catch: 3-5 reps per set, every rep fast
   - Olympic lifts are for power: no percentages unless your coach switches them on
   > Full squat catch.
   - Swap for: Power Clean / Squat Clean / Power Clean & Jerk / Low-Hang Clean Pull
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Squat%20Clean%20%26%20Jerk)

**Strength**

   - Swap for: Box Squat - any variation, dead-stop ('Deload' = sit and unload on the box) / Low-Bar Back Squat / High-Bar Back Squat / Zercher Squat (barbell)

   - Swap for: Weighted Pull-Up / Chin-Up (supinated) / L-Sit Pull-Up / Strict Pull-Up (Knees to Chest / tuck)

## Week 12 — Hardest weeks

_Effort level this week: 11 (2 = start, 11 = a little past your limits)_

**End test this week: replaces this week's top sets** (Strength, Speed / agility, Jumps)

_last week: intensifiers allowed (MYO set on the last machine / isolation exercise)_

**What changes this week**
- +1 set on Side (Lateral) Lunge (Thursday)
- +1 set on Machine Fly (Pec Deck) (Friday)
- +1 set on Bent-Over Barbell Row (Thursday)
- +1 set on Machine Calf Raise (Friday)
- Quads is at its weekly ceiling: add load or reps, not sets
- Chest is at its weekly ceiling: add load or reps, not sets
- Back (lats / upper back) is at its weekly ceiling: add load or reps, not sets
- Calves is at its weekly ceiling: add load or reps, not sets
- Same sets: add a little load or reps this week

**Athletic work this week:** 140 jump contacts · 108 throws · 96 direction changes · 400 m of sprints · 12 Olympic-lift sets · Plyo share 36% · Receive-Create 3: Repeat

### Monday · A1 - squat + pull
_about 101 min_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **A-March (running drill)**

1. **Sit-Up** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Copenhagen Plank

   - Swap for: Hanging Rotational Knees-to-Chest (hanging oblique knee raise) / Toes to Bar (hanging) / Knees to Chest (hanging) / Hanging Rotational Knee/Leg Raise

**Test**
1. 10-30 m sprint — 10-30 m sprint · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - **Test week:** 10-30 m sprint
   - Test: 2-3 timed runs with full rest, record the best.
2. **5-10-5 Pro-Agility Shuttle** — 5-10-5 Pro-Agility Shuttle · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - **Test week:** 5-10-5 Pro-Agility Shuttle
   - Test: 2-3 timed runs with full rest, record the best.
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=5-10-5%20Pro-Agility%20Shuttle)
3. countermovement jump — countermovement jump · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - **Test week:** countermovement jump
   - Test: 3 attempts, record the best.

**Jumps, throws and speed**
1. **Max-Velocity Sprint (Top Speed)** — 2 × 5 x 40 m · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Swap for: A-Skip (running drill) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill) / A-March (running drill) / Falling-Start Accelerations

2. **Single-Leg Depth Jump** — 3 × 8 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · 2-3 min rest

   - per side = 1 set

   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Single-Leg%20Depth%20Jump)
3. **Bilateral Zig-Zag Ankle Hops** — 2 × 25 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · work:rest 1:5 to 1:10
   - Repeat: quick, continuous contacts
   - Swap for: Bilateral Right-Angle Ankle Hops (Forward-Right-Forward-Left-Forward) / Ankle Hops (bilateral, in place) / Bilateral Backward Ankle Hops / Bilateral Lateral Ankle Hops
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Bilateral%20Zig-Zag%20Ankle%20Hops)
4. **Step-Forward Medball Overhead Throw (R & L leg)** — 3 × 18 Reps · max intent; stop when quality drops · 45-90 s rest
   - 6-10 lb ball
   > No levels: just do them right (quality and intent over load).

**Power / Olympic lifts**
1. **Squat Clean & Jerk** — 3 × 3 to 5 Reps · move it fast: if the bar slows down or your technique breaks, it is too heavy · 2-3 min rest

   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Alternating DB Overhead Press / Half-Kneeling Single-Arm Landmine Press / Standing Barbell Overhead Press (strict) / Barbell Push Press
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seesaw%20DB%20Press%20(alternating%2C%20one%20up%20%2F%20one%20down))
4. **Strict Pull-Up (Knees to Chest / tuck)** — 3 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ DB Leg Extension (seated on box)
   - Body-weight lift: if you cannot reach the reps, use the assisted machine or a band-free regression (feet on the floor, ring or TRX row).
   - Swap for: L-Sit Pull-Up / Weighted Pull-Up / Strict Pull-Up (Feet Behind) / Wide-Grip Pull-Up
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Strict%20Pull-Up%20(Knees%20to%20Chest%20%2F%20tuck))

**Accessories**
1. **DB Leg Extension (seated on box)** — 2 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Strict Pull-Up (Knees to Chest / tuck)
   - Swap for: Sissy Squat / Leg Extension (machine)
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/HpCE66lfEuk)
2. **Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly** — 4 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: Seated (Chest-to-Thighs) DB Reverse Fly / KB Face Pull / Floor Prone Y / T Raises (bodyweight or light DBs) / Face Pull
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Bent-Over%20DB%20Reverse%20Fly)
3. **Standing Calf Raise (bodyweight, from a deficit/step)** — 4 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: Barbell Calf Raise / Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) / Single-Leg Deficit Calf Raise (weighted) / Machine Calf Raise
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Standing%20Calf%20Raise%20(bodyweight%2C%20from%20a%20deficit%2Fstep))
4. **Machine Reverse Fly (rear-delt pec deck) (bonus)** — 3 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · MYO set on the last set · 60-90 s rest
   - Bonus exercise to reach your weekly sets
   - Swap for: Seated (Chest-to-Thighs) DB Reverse Fly / KB Face Pull / Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly / Floor Prone Y / T Raises (bodyweight or light DBs)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Machine%20Reverse%20Fly%20(rear-delt%20pec%20deck))

### Friday · B2 - deadlift + push
_about 70 min_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **A-March (running drill)**
   - Swap for: A-Skip (running drill) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=A-March%20(running%20drill))

**Core**
1. **Cable Woodchopper (cable chop)** — 2 × 10 to 15 per side · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 30-60 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Side Plank (elbow) / Weighted Russian Twist / Russian Twist / Cable Oblique Rotation (cable rotational twist)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Cable%20Woodchopper%20(cable%20chop))
2. **Reverse Crunch** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 30-60 s rest
   - Swap for: Copenhagen Plank / Knees to Chest (hanging) / Toes to Bar (hanging) / Hanging Rotational Knee/Leg Raise
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Reverse%20Crunch)

**Jumps, throws and speed**
1. **Single-Leg Forward Hurdle Hops** — 3 × 8 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · work:rest 1:5 to 1:10
   - Repeat: quick, continuous contacts
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Hurdle Hops (tall hurdles, continuous quick-contact reps) / Single-Leg Plate Hops / DB Ankle Hops / Single-Leg Squirrel Jumps
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Single-Leg%20Forward%20Hurdle%20Hops)
2. **Slant Route** — 3 × 12 cuts · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Swap for: 5-10-5 Pro-Agility Shuttle / Fade Route / Lateral Shuffles / L-Test / L-Drill (3-Cone)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Slant%20Route)

**Power / Olympic lifts**
1. **Squat Snatch** — 3 × 3 to 5 Reps · move it fast: if the bar slows down or your technique breaks, it is too heavy · 2-3 min rest
   - weight by speed: fast and powerful
   - Full squat catch: 3-5 reps per set, every rep fast
   - Olympic lifts are for power: no percentages unless your coach switches them on
   > Full squat catch.
   - Swap for: Low-Hang Snatch Pull / Power Snatch / Single-Arm DB Snatch / Low-Hang Squat Snatch
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Squat%20Snatch)

**Strength**
1. **Stiff-Leg Deadlift / RDL** — 3 × 3 rep max or work up to a 1 rep max · max intent; stop when quality drops · 3-6 min rest
   - **Test week:** 3 rep max or work up to a 1 rep max
   - Test: work up to one top set of 3 rep max or work up to a 1 rep max (warm-up sets first), then record the weight.
   - Test day: replaces this week's top sets
   - Swap for: Hex (Trap) Bar Deadlift / Sumo Deadlift / Barbell Deadlift (conventional) / Snatch-Grip Barbell Deadlift
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Stiff-Leg%20Deadlift%20%2F%20RDL)
2. **Incline Barbell Bench Press** — 3 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-120 s rest
   - Swap for: Barbell Bench Press / Alternating Incline DB Bench Press (one up / one down) / Alternating Flat DB Bench Press / Alternating DB Floor Press
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Incline%20Barbell%20Bench%20Press)

**Accessories**
1. **Machine Leg Curl (seated or lying)** — 3 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Machine Fly (Pec Deck)
   - Swap for: Nordic Hamstring Curl / Prone Dumbbell Hamstring Curl (DB between feet) / Ball Hamstring Curl (medball or stability ball) / Seated Leg Curl (machine)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Machine%20Leg%20Curl%20(seated%20or%20lying))
2. **Machine Fly (Pec Deck)** — 3 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · rest after the pair · SUPERSET W/ Machine Leg Curl (seated or lying)
   - Swap for: Flat DB Fly / Cable Fly
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Machine%20Fly%20(Pec%20Deck))
3. **Machine Calf Raise** — 4 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-90 s rest
   - Swap for: Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) / Barbell Calf Raise / Single-Leg Deficit Calf Raise (weighted) / Seated / Bent-Knee Calf Raise (soleus; machine or DBs on the knees)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Machine%20Calf%20Raise)
4. **Single-Leg Hip Thrust (shoulders on bench) (Focus)** — 2 × 8 to 12 per side · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · MYO set on the last set · 60-90 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Extra exercise to reach your weekly sets
   - Swap for: Reverse Hyperextension / Hip Thrust or Glute Bridge (either) / Glute Bridge / Sandbag Hip Thrust
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Single-Leg%20Hip%20Thrust%20(shoulders%20on%20bench))

### Saturday · Optional day (optional)
_about 45 min_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio

**Core**
1. **Weighted Russian Twist** — 2 × 10 to 15 per side · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 30-60 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Russian Twist / Cable Oblique Rotation (cable rotational twist) / Hanging Rotational Knees-to-Chest (hanging oblique knee raise) / Cable Woodchopper (cable chop)
   - [Video](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4p5jWyz-YRQ)

**Accessories**
1. **Floor Prone Y / T Raises (bodyweight or light DBs) (Focus)** — 3 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-90 s rest
   - Extra exercise to reach your weekly sets
   - Swap for: Seated (Chest-to-Thighs) DB Reverse Fly / Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly / KB Face Pull / Machine Reverse Fly (rear-delt pec deck)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Floor%20Prone%20Y%20%2F%20T%20Raises%20(bodyweight%20or%20light%20DBs))
2. **Reverse Hyperextension (Focus)** — 3 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · MYO set on the last set · 60-90 s rest
   - Extra exercise to reach your weekly sets
   > Use if available and the client feels the right muscles.
   - Swap for: Barbell Hip Thrust / Single-Leg Hip Thrust (shoulders on bench) / Cable Pull-Through / Dumbbell Hip Thrust
   - [Video](https://youtu.be/3vmbvoT2m-U)

**Aerobic**
1. **Aerobic B (green): 2 Minute run/ 4 Min Walk** — Green (B): moderate · 20-30 min
   - Any cardio tool works: run, bike, rower or ski erg. Track time, calories or distance.
   - This cardio session is part of the plan even if you skip the optional lifting.
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Aerobic%20B%20(green)%3A%202%20Minute%20run%2F%204%20Min%20Walk)
